- 2026-07-14 | pair-1 data-quality audit | RabbitMQ PerfTest 2.25.0 applies --rate per producer. Q3 uses 8 producers but passes the intended total rate to each producer, so the nominal 50/70/85 percent points are all saturated and must not be interpreted as a load curve. Current-campaign Q3 data can only be retained as redundant saturation runs; no active remote script was changed mid-campaign.
- 2026-07-14 | pair-1 data-quality audit | RabbitMQ Q5 uses unsupported --producer-msg-count and --consumer-msg-count options. PerfTest prints Parsing failed but exits 0, no formal perftest.csv is produced, and the harness incorrectly creates VALID. Pair 1 Q5 has been excluded from analysis. The active campaign remains unchanged for cross-pair consistency; Q5 requires a corrected paired rerun with -C/-D plus queue-count validation.
